# 3D financial charts (graphics.el)

Generated by `make golden-3d` (`financial-chart-3d-write-gallery`); do not
edit by hand.

Every financial-chart template has a 3D variant in `templates3d/`: the
template with its body unchanged plus an `x-eas.graphics` naming a
[graphics.el](https://gitlab.com/davidawad/graphics-el) mark
(graphics.el's `docs/design/eas-integration.md`).
`src/financial-chart-3d.el` registers them with eas under the namespace
`fc3d`, so `fc3d/ohlc` is the variant of `ohlc`. eas still draws each in
2D; graphics.el draws it in 3D.

graphics.el is optional: without it everything 2D works as before and the
3D commands signal `financial-chart-3d-unavailable`, saying how to install
it.

```elisp
(require 'financial-chart-3d)
(financial-chart-3d-open "ohlc" :show t)                 ; its example
(financial-chart-3d-open "ohlc" :bindings '(:bars BARS) :show t)
M-x financial-chart-3d-gallery     ; every variant, a grid of windows
M-x financial-chart-3d-demo-book   ; the order-book surface, streaming
(setq v (financial-chart-3d-book-open SNAPSHOT :show t))  ; live book
(financial-chart-3d-book-push v DELTAS)
```

In a 3D buffer the arrows orbit, `+`/`-` zoom and `0` resets; a replay
plays and pauses with `SPC`, steps with `,` and `.` and changes speed
with `[` and `]`.

Marks: `bar3d` and `depth3d` are graphics.el's own. The `fc-` marks are
registered by `financial-chart-3d.el` with `graphics-eas-define-mark`:
candles whose scales span the whole series (so a replay adds bars in
place), stacked panes, a volume profile beside candles, the underwater
curve, a payoff heightfield, depth walls and sign-coloured bars.

Each render below is the variant's golden (`test/golden/3d/NAME.txt`): its
example at 60x24 cells, dark palette, the default camera, at the end of
its replay, drawn by the Rust core. The Braille text keeps the dots, not
the colours. `make test-3d` checks every variant against its template and
draws its golden bit for bit.

| variant | mark | replay | 3D reading |
|---|---|---|---|
| [cds-term-structure](#cds-term-structure) | `fc-cds-term3d` | yes | the term structures as a surface of lines over tenor and date, coloured by level; replays date by date, the earlier ones left as a trail, so a stress that flattens the front end stands out |
| [credit-spreads](#credit-spreads) | `fc-credit3d` |  | spread surface over tenor and rating as a wire mesh, AAA in front, log height, coloured by each spread's percentile in its own history (green tight, red wide) |
| [dcf-sensitivity](#dcf-sensitivity) | `fc-dcf3d` | yes | value per share as a surface over WACC and terminal growth, the current price a plane through it and the fair-value line where they cross; snapshots morph one into the next |
| [depth-live](#depth-live) | `depth3d` |  | order-book surface: cumulative depth by price (across) and step (back), streaming live from financial-chart-3d-book-push |
| [depth](#depth) | `fc-depth3d` |  | cumulative depth as two walls stepping away from the mid, bids in front, asks behind |
| [diverging-bars](#diverging-bars) | `fc-diverging3d` |  | one 3D bar per label, up from the floor for gains and down through it for losses |
| [drawdown](#drawdown) | `fc-drawdown3d` | yes | the underwater curve as a slab hanging below the high-water line, seen from below; replays point by point |
| [greeks-surface](#greeks-surface) | `fc-greeks3d` | yes | the greek as a surface over spot and days to expiry, the slice at the current spot traced; replays toward expiry |
| [kelly-surface](#kelly-surface) | `fc-kelly3d` |  | log growth as a surface over bet fraction and edge, the Kelly fraction traced along its ridge and the zero-growth line where over-betting starts to lose |
| [key-rate-durations](#key-rate-durations) | `fc-krd3d` |  | a bar per holding and key rate, as tall as its duration (short exposure below the floor), each holding its own colour |
| [ladder](#ladder) | `bar3d` |  | the price ladder as 3D bars, one per level, bids and asks in two lanes; live pushes grow and shrink them |
| [ohlc](#ohlc) | `fc-candle3d` | yes | candles as boxes along time, price up, each body as deep as its volume; replays bar by bar |
| [options-gex](#options-gex) | `fc-strikes3d` |  | dealer gamma as 3D bars on a strike by expiry floor, call gamma up and put gamma down, spot a plane across the strikes and the gamma flip (where net gamma by strike changes sign) a violet one |
| [options-oi](#options-oi) | `fc-strikes3d` |  | open interest as 3D bars on a strike by expiry floor, calls rising and puts hanging below it, spot a plane across the strikes and each expiry's max-pain strike a gold post |
| [options-skew](#options-skew) | `fc-skew3d` | yes | risk reversal (top) and butterfly (bottom) as ribbons over time, one per tenor (shortest in front), the newest term structure traced bright across them; replays snapshot by snapshot |
| [options-vrp](#options-vrp) | `fc-vrp3d` | yes | implied and realized volatility as the two edges of one ribbon over time, filled green where implied is rich and red where realized overtakes it, IV rank as bars behind and the front/back IV ratio in front; replays day by day |
| [panes](#panes) | `fc-panes3d` | yes | price, volume and RSI(14) as three stacked panels on one time axis; replays bar by bar |
| [payoff-curves](#payoff-curves) | `fc-payoff3d` | yes | the curves as one P/L surface over price and curve (a strategy at several dates: a surface toward expiry); builds curve by curve |
| [payoff](#payoff) | `fc-payoff3d` | yes | P/L surface over price and days to expiry, the earlier days priced from the expiry payoff; it decays row by row to expiry |
| [pop-surface](#pop-surface) | `fc-pop3d` |  | the probability as a surface over strike and expiry, red at 0 to green at 1, the even-odds level framed |
| [position-pnl-surface](#position-pnl-surface) | `fc-pnl3d` | yes | P/L surface over spot and days to expiry with the expiry payoff as its front edge; decays day by day toward it |
| [revenue-surface](#revenue-surface) | `fc-revenue3d` | yes | revenue as a surface over fiscal year and outcome quantile, the cone widening with the horizon and the median traced; it grows year by year |
| [risk-contribution](#risk-contribution) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one ribbon per asset, its height that asset's share of portfolio volatility; replays date by date |
| [risk-correlation](#risk-correlation) | `fc-risk-grid3d` | yes | the correlation matrix as signed 3D bars on an asset by asset floor (up green for positive, down red for negative), animated window by window so the bars rise and sink as correlations shift |
| [risk-factor-exposure](#risk-factor-exposure) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one ribbon per factor around a zero plane, long exposures standing above it and short ones hanging below; replays date by date |
| [risk-frontier](#risk-frontier) | `fc-risk-frontier3d` | yes | the frontiers as one surface of return over volatility (across) and date (back), coloured by Sharpe, each date's maximum-Sharpe portfolio marked; grows date by date |
| [risk-return-distribution](#risk-return-distribution) | `fc-risk-histogram3d` | yes | one histogram per horizon in lanes from front to back on shared return bins, loss bins red and gain bins green, each lane's 95% VaR ruled across it; builds horizon by horizon |
| [risk-stress](#risk-stress) | `fc-risk-grid3d` |  | the scenario by position grid as signed 3D bars, losses sinking red through the floor and gains rising green, a net column per scenario |
| [risk-underwater](#risk-underwater) | `fc-risk-ribbons3d` | yes | one slab per strategy hanging below its own high-water line, a ridgeline as deep as each drawdown, the worst point of each marked; replays week by week |
| [vol-surface](#vol-surface) | `fc-vol-surface3d` | yes | IV surface over strike and days to expiry as a mesh of each expiry's smile and the strikes' term structures, coloured by IV, the at-the-money term structure traced across it; replays the session quote time by quote time while the camera sways |
| [volume-profile](#volume-profile) | `fc-volume-profile3d` | yes | horizontal volume-at-price bars beside the candles, point of control bright; the profile builds as the bars replay |
| [yield-curve](#yield-curve) | `fc-yield-curve3d` | yes | the fitted curves as a surface of lines over tenor and date, each coloured by its regime (teal upward, amber flat, red inverted); replays one curve per frame, the earlier ones left as a trail, the forward curve beside the last |

## risk-correlation

`fc3d/risk-correlation`, mark `fc-risk-grid3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/risk/risk-correlation.json`. The correlation matrix as signed 3D bars on an asset by asset floor (up green for positive, down red for negative), animated window by window so the bars rise and sink as correlations shift.

`fc3d/volume-profile`, mark `fc-volume-profile3d`, example `examples/volume-profile.data.json`. Horizontal volume-at-price bars beside the candles, point of control bright; the profile builds as the bars replay.

## yield-curve

`fc3d/yield-curve`, mark `fc-yield-curve3d`, example `test/fixtures/3d/rates/yield-curve.data.json`. The fitted curves as a surface of lines over tenor and date, each coloured by its regime (teal upward, amber flat, red inverted); replays one curve per frame, the earlier ones left as a trail, the forward curve beside the last.
